Just because we are Muslims or we are Sulemani's we start thinking we're better than the rest of the world! Yes we are definitely superior in our knowledge of realities - AlHamdulillah! No doubt about that! But that doesn't give us the right to treat other communities or other religions with spite. It does not give us the license to look down upon others. Do you understand what I'm saying? In today's world we are afflicted with this disease of superiority and this feeling of having a chip on our shoulders whether we admit to it or not. Whether it's by religion, by race, by money, by social status by whatever reason. Don't forget we all have the same رب.
 Superiority in ranks can only exist through the eyes of the master - based on the servitude of the slave! Not through the eyes of the slave.

إِنَّ أَكْرَمَكُمْ عِندَ اللَّهِ أَتْقَاكُمْ

The most honorable in the eyes of Allah. is the one who is the most pious. That's it. Not by anything else. Because even the highest ranking person is Allah's slave, and even the lowest ranking person is Allah's save. Look what we recite in each and every أذان . What do we say? 5 times a day?

اشھد ان محمد عبدہ و رسولہ

I testify that Muhammad ﷺ is his **slave** and his messenger. AllahuAkbar. The highest ranking person who has ever walked this earth - we testify in every prayer, that he is the slave of Allah! Then what right do we have to claim superiority over each other? If Allah has blessed us with some gifts - and what's the greatest gift of all? ایمان! It is faith! Then that gift should make us more humble - not arrogant!

Does Quran tell us the story of someone who became arrogant about his title and his rank? Do you remember the story of ابلیس?

أَنَا۠ خَيْرٌۭ مِّنْهُ . خَلَقْتَنِى مِن نَّارٍۢ وَخَلَقْتَهُۥ مِن طِينٍۢ

I am better than him. I am created from fire! He is created from clay. Who was ابلیس! He was was an angel, right? From the عالَم of angels. Adam was a human being. from the عالَم of انسان. They both were slaves of Allah. And ابلیس stood before Allah; He stood before the رَبُّ الْعَالَمِینَ and said:

أَنَا۠ خَيْرٌۭ مِّنْهُ خَلَقْتَنِى مِن نَّارٍۢ وَخَلَقْتَهُۥ مِن طِينٍۢ

I am better than him. And what do we learn in that story. What did his master do?. Did his arrogance bring him closer to Allah. or did it get him removed from his divine presence? Do you really think that an act that ابلیس was punished for, Allah would let it slide for you and I? Would that befit his justice? That he punishes ابلیس for his arrogance; remove him from his divine presence, but then reward us bring us closer to him? Impossible!

Because you have to understand! When you and I judge each other! When you and I as slaves judge other slaves in terms of superiority - what position do we place ourselves in? We put ourselves in the position of our رب. Now does the Quran also tell us of someone who did that? Didn't someone say. اَنَا رَبُّکُمُ الْاَعْلَی. I am your greatest رب. Who said that? فرعون said that. How did he end up? And the reality is that فرعون knew that he was human. He knew he was not a رب. He was fully aware of his weakness; but despite that he claimed a station he was not worthy of. Did you know, he did not like the fact that he needed to go to the toilet. Because that was not befitting a رب. So he got all of his physicians together and he asked them how do I get out of this problem? So they told him to eat bananas. So he ate bananas for 40 days; and then he ended up with a much bigger problem.

The reason I'm repeating this and enforcing this so much because today, this is not just a Fironic problem. This has become a GLOBAL problem. Everyone wants to become الہ ; everyone wants to become معبود. At some level we have all become people who want to be worshiped; but we forget how Allah has defined us in the Quran:

وَلَقَدْ خَلَقْنَا ٱلْإِنسَنَ مِن سُلَلَةٍۢ مِّن طِينٍۢ

You are from some chain of dirt; of mud and water mixed together.

ثُمَّ جَعَلَ نَسْلَهُۥ مِن سُلَلَةٍۢ مِّن مَّآءٍۢ مَّهِينٍۢ

We created your progeny from a vial fluid. Who do you think you are? Allah tells these people. Get down off your high horse!!!. Be a رجل. A رجل is the one that has ِرَجَلَ. Who as they say - ِرَجَلَ عن فَرِسِہ. He is the one who comes down off his high horse, and puts his feet on the ground. Doesn't see himself raised up above the earth. He comes down to the level that he came from - from dust. That's الرجل. But we're all people, who at some level are just playing games - trying to be الہ . and Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى says NO! Be a رجل. Be بنی آدم ! Be آدمی . That is truly accepting Allah as رَبُّ الْعَالَمِینَ. Humility, not arrogance is the trademark of a believer.

The acceptance of our عبودیۃ , our slavery and servitude to Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى is a heavy burden that we as believer carry on our backs! When you carry a burden on your back, and you try to climb a mountain - do you bend forward or do you walk upright? The burden makes you bend; it makes you humble - doesn't it? And this is why HUMILITY is the essence of spiritual progress. Maulana Ali says

مَنْ تَوَاضَعَ لِی اللَّہُ، رَفَعَ اللَّہُ لَہُ

The one who becomes humble for Allah, Allah raises him and elevates him in ranks. This is why, the highest ranking person to every walk the face of this earth, was my beloved prophet مُحَمَّدٌ مُصْطَفَی ﷺ. The greatest ranking man that this world will ever know was the most humble in his character. In fact, he was marked by humility! All previous prophecies that predicted his arrival, they identified him as being the greatest in his humility. SubhanAllah! The greatest mark . THE GREATEST MARK of his prophet hood was his humility!

یُریدُ أَنَّ یَبعَثُ أُمِیِّ مِنَ الأُمِّیِّن۔وأجعل السكينةَ لباسُهُ ، والبِرَّ شَعارُهُ ، والتقوى ضَميرَهُ ، والحِكمَةَ مَنطِقَهُ ، والصِّدقَ والوفاءَ طَبِيعَتُهُ ، والعَفوَ وَالمَعرُوفَ خُلقُهُ ، والَحقَّ شَريعَتَهُ ، والعَدلَ سيرَتَهُ ، والهَدِىُ إمامُهُ ، والإسلامَ مِلَّتُهُ ، وأحمد أِسمُهُ . أَهدِي بِهِ بَعدَ الضَّلالَة ، وأعلَمُ بِهِ مِنَ الجَهالة ، وأَرفَعُ به بعد الخَمالَة ، وأعرِفُ به بعد النَّكِرَة ، وأكثَرُ بِهِ بعدَ القِلَّة ، وأغنِي به بعد العَيلَة ، وأَجمِعُ به بعد الفُرقَة

This is the prediction of Rasul Allah ﷺ's arrival in the bible! Allah mentions all these beautiful signs of his arrival:

I am going to send you a man from your own nation - his garment will be a peaceful temperament; his behavior will be kind; his conscience will be pious; his logic will be wise; honesty and truth will be his nature; his dealings will be full of forgiveness; justice will be his actions, Ahmed will be his name

أَهدِي بِهِ بَعدَ الضَّلالَة ، وأعلَمُ بِهِ مِنَ الجَهالة ، وأَرفَعُ به بعد الخَمالَة ، وأعرِفُ به بعد النَّكِرَة ، وأكثَرُ بِهِ بعدَ القِلَّة ، وأغنِي به بعد العَيلَة

He will guide a nation and bring them out of error; he will give them knowledge to bring them out of ignorance; he will be raised after being belittled; he will become known after being unknown; he will gain abundance after scarcity; he will become wealthy after poverty; Allah goes on mentioning all these traits about his beloved. But then after revealing all these beautiful traits and incredible signs, he mentions the greatest sign of all !

لَو یَمشِی عَلی القَصَبِ الیَابِس۔۔۔ لَم یُسمَع مِن تَحتِ قَدَمَیہِ مِن سَکِینَتِہِ

He will be so humble! He will walk among you in such humility, that when he walks on dry leaves, you will not hear the sound of them cracking under his feet. SubhanAllah! This is the prophet we follow! This is the example we follow!

If you want to know why this slave of Allah gained the honor he achieved from his master, it is through this one trait ! HUMILITY!. And that is what identifies a true believer from a hypocrite! HUMILITY. This is why I feel atheism is such a blatant form of arrogance. Because the atheist is rejecting something that is the most obvious - and the most fundamental aspect of his existence. That he was **created**! Because even he cannot say that he was not created. Now if I say that my father created me and and his father created him before him. That's what they call دور that's تسلسل. It just keeps on going back. And then where do you stop? It stops at Adam! Who created Adam? Because that's where the buck stops.

وکُلُّکُم مِن آدَم. وَ آدَم مِن تُرَاب

All of you are from Adam, and Adam is from dust. And arrogance doesn't befit the dirt, because it is constantly trampled upon!

Maulana Ali saw a man acting arrogantly and he said to him. Who do you think you are?. You beginning is a vile dirty fluid. You end is disgusting decaying smelling corpse. And in between those two stages. you walk through life carrying two loathsome thing within your body. your urine and your feces. And your creation is the result of the union of two ugly body parts. What right do you have to act arrogantly before Allah and his creation?.

And so this is the first thing. to know our station in this world. to know who we are. We are Marboob. We are created. We will die. We will be raised up. THIS understanding. that we are Marboob. THIS is the station of the slave. of knowing that I am NOT Mabood…. I am not the creator….

Wallahi I give myself, and I give you sincere advise - be humble in all walks of lives. Do not become arrogant! It is THE greatest challenge we all face in our times today. Arrogance is the أذان; It is the call of our times today. Arrogance has become the trademark of success in this world today - in schools, in colleges, at work, in families, in communities - It's all about I am better than you! Arrogance does not befit an obedient slave.

May Allah grant us all humility and guide us, enable us in walking in the footsteps of our beloved prophet ﷺ.

If we just get this bit. If we really understand the true meaning of Rabb AlAlameen. this world would become such a beautiful place. If we all understood that it is Allah the Rabb Alalmeen who guides everyone. he is the king. he is the owner. he is the one who cares. he is the one who guides. he is the one who gives gifts. then everything about my life is between me and him. He will take care of me. I just need to worry about anything. I don't need to judge another slave. I don't need to hurt another slave. I don't need to show my authority or power over another slave. EVEN if I am hurt. EVEN if I am wronged. ESPECIALLY if I have been wronged. Because my master will take care of me. My master will come for my rescue. he will come to my defense on the day of judgment. And he WILL make that wrong right. He will stand behind me and support me. I don't need to worry about anything. Because wheter we believe it or not. wheter we want to or not. that day is coming. when the Rabb will gather everyone and exact justice.
رَبَّنَآ إِنَّكَ جَامِعُ ٱلنَّاسِ لِيَوْمٍۢ لَّا رَيْبَ فِيهِ ۚ
 Oh our master. Indeed you will gather all of us on a day. about which there is ABSOLUTELY NO DOUBT. Because YOU have promised us that.
إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَا يُخْلِفُ ٱلْمِيعَادَ
 No doubt. Allah never goes against his promise.
 Remind yourself of this EVERY time you recite the Fateha. There's a reason we pray 51 rakaats in one day. and all 51 times we recite Fateha. So that every time we say Rabb Alalameen. we remind ourselves of this hierarchy. Who He is and who we are in this relationship.

وآخر الدعوانا عن الحمد للہ رَبُّ الْعَالَمِینَ

Man gave Rasul Allah bottle of wine as gift

There was this man who travelled a long distance to meet Rasul Allah (Salawaat). and when he met him. he gave the messenger a bottle of wine. as a gift. He said Ya RasulAllah. I got this special wine as a gift for you. What would we do if someone did that?. If someone. very secularized. shows up at your doorstep with a bottle of wine. as a house warming gift. what how would we react?. La hawla wala quwwata. How dare you. Don't you know I'm a Muslim. Don't you know this is haraam?. Get the hell out of my house. never show me your face again. right?.
 And who did this man bring the wine bottle to? . Rasul Allah!!!!. The MOST pious man we can imagine. And you know how he responded?. He smiled. He smiled and said. Brother. Did you not hear Allah's command that alcohol has been prohibited?. He said. No Rasul Allah. I never heard that!. He immediately gives the bottle to his slave and says something in his ears. Rasul Allah says. what did you just say to him?. The man said. I told him to sell the bottle in the market. Rasul Allah smiled again and said. The one who has prohibitted the consumption of intoxicants has also forbidden its trade. So the man told the slave to dump it out. This is the character of Rasul Allah. This is his humility. This is the Tarbiyyah. his rububiyyah. SubhanAllah! . He didn't say. Don't you know who I am. How dare you bring this filth in my presence. He NEVER did that. Because hearts can NEVER be conquered with harshness. Hearts have always been conquered ONLY with love. and kindness. We should really study. and learn what kind of a Rabb. what kind of a parent. what kind of teacher our prophet ﷺ was. SubhanAllah.

## The Divine Formula For Recognizing Allah

In the previous session we looked at the term عالَم; which is anything and everything that can be known by the عالِم to enable him to recognize the علیم. Because you need the presence of an object before you can acquire knowledge about it. In order to learn about human physiology, we need a human body. For Mathematics we need numbers. We need physicality to exist before we can study physics. As human beings, we cannot gain knowledge of a subject without the object for that study. So this عالَم is the object that gives us knowledge about the علیم.

We then looked at the statement رَبُّ الْعَالَمِینَ to understand that Allah is not just the master of this universe, but of everything that can be known by the human intellect. Therefore everything that exists in creation is a reflection of the علیم; whether it is physical, meta-physical, conceptual, imaginable - no matter what it is, it reflects the علیم. So for example, the fact that he created this infinite universe just for us, reflects not just his glory and magnificence, but also his generosity and the vastness of his رحمۃ.

### Divine Formula

Now to further elaborate on that, and to put it in perspective, there are two fundamental questions that surround the understanding of God. The first is Who is God? And the second is What does he want from us? Those are the two essential questions that mankind has grappled with from the beginning of time. Who is God, and what does he want from us?

The first question of who is god, includes questions like: What are his capabilities? What are his attributes? His personality? His traits and so on. The second question of what does he want from us include questions such as Why did he create this universe? What is our purpose on this earth? What are his expectations from us etc. To answer both these questions, Allah gives a very simple and straight forward formula through a verse in the Quran. He says:

Question

![](images/81/cd8a1733-7cb4-4e8a-a67a-cbd7e47ed8f0.jpg)

He mentions two broad categories in this verse. The horizons and the soul. What is the horizon? It is everything that exists outside of us - this physical universe. What is the soul? It is everything that exists inside of us - our spiritual universe. Yes? So how does Allah answer the question, Who am I?

Answers

![](images/81/b813ade8-e959-40c9-8d6a-4fc1a4737099.jpg)

He answers it through the horizons - this physical universe, and everything that exists within it. How does he answer the question, "What is it that I want from you? ". He answers it through the نفس - through our own creation and existence - through the creation of our spirit and soul. And this is what I refer to as the genius of Allah's design. I for one, find it incredibly amazing how he answers such a complex and impossible question using such a simple formula.

So just keep this formula in mind as I explain the details and you'll see how amazing this solution is - at least it is to me. In my personal opinion, this is my absolute proof of the existence of God and Quran being the message of Allah - because this kind of perfection in design simply cannot exist at the human level.

And this is also Allah's solution to the problem that theology faces in modern days about the existence of god. The problems today is that we are try to invent and apply our own formulas to prove Allah's existence. But the fact of the matter is that he has already given us his formula in the Quran, which is a simple solution to derive at that conclusions. Because it is the only formula that will work. If we try to apply our own formulas, it is simply not going to work - because this universe is his kingdom, his design. So we have to follow his rules. Again, this is something we can find plenty of examples for in this world. You have to use the iPhone according to Apple's specifications. If you try to use it any other way it's not going to work. So Allah's formula of his recognition is the آفاق and the أنفس.

Now let's break it down and look at each component in more detail. I'm going to get a bit technical here, so bear with me.

### Horizons

Let's start with the first part of the formula - the آفاق, the horizons? What questions does it answer?

- Who is Allah?

- What is he capable of?

- Why is he even worthy of my worship, my willing slavery and servitude.

- What makes him so glorious or magnificent or incredible in my eyes that I would even think of considering him as my master?

Allah answers those questions using the signs in the آفاق - His signs in the horizon or in this universe. I covered this topic in one of the prior sessions of "Conclusions of reflection" session. In that we looked at the pre-requisite for understanding this formula. Which is that:

1. You must have a sound intellect and a pure heart, which the Quran refers to as لُب; the core. The Quran refers to such people as اُولُو الْاَلْبَابِ - people of pure hearts and sound intellects. Because it is the combination of the two that allows us to perceive reality as it truly is.

1. The second requirement of the Quran and this formula is an uncontaminated, pure فطرۃ, which stems from a pure heart and a sound intellect.

The device that allows us to study the universe and learn about it, is the intellect - العقل and the way it works is by defining and limiting things. Intellect is the container, and knowledge is the content that is placed within that container. Now what knowledge (عِلْم)? Very simply defined, knowledge is the understanding and the recognizing the true reality of things.

Concept and judgment

![](images/81/5f48abc4-58c6-4794-9d67-7a23731cf232.jpg)

The foundation of that understanding first involves a concept, which is تصور in Arabic. It is a conceptualization of that reality, which is then followed by a حکم a ruling or judgment about the authenticity of that reality. The Arabs say:

الحُکمُ علی شَیء فرحٌ عَن تَصَوُّرِہِ

Before you can judge a thing you must define it, and understand it in all of its comprehensible dimensions. Then once you conceptualize that reality you can pass a judgment on whether it is true for false; good or bad etc.

Propositions

![](images/81/04fc84c0-c8c1-4ef9-a632-89eb104a4873.jpg)

Now how do we as human beings do that? How do we conceptualize things? Scholars say that there are 3 types of propositions. First of all what is a proposition? Let's start there... A proposition is a statement or an assertion that expresses a judgment or an opinion. So I propose to you that "fire burns", or "fire is bright". Now you take that proposition and based on your observations, you conclude whether that statement is true or false. That's how a proposition works. Scholars say that there are 3 types of propositions:

- Empirical Propositions

- Rational Propositions

- Philosophical/Revelatory Propositions

### Empirical Propositions

The first are the "empirical propositions", which are based on our observation or experience, utilizing the 5 senses (sight, hearing, smell, taste, touch). These are also called sensory propositions. These deal with the realm that can be witnessed [which the Quran calls عَالَمُ الشَّھَادَۃِ]. So when I propose to you that fire is hot, it is bright, when it combines with oxygen, combustion takes place, these are all empirical or sensory propositions, right? Because you can observe the validity of these propositions using your 5 senses.

### Rational Propositions

The second are "rational propositions", which are logical propositions, like 3 + 2 equals 5, therefore 5 - 3 must be 2. These propositions work through inference. If I see smoke rising in the distance, I can infer that there is a fire there, even though I am unable to see the fire itself, you see? Based on my prior empirical knowledge that when something burns, it emits smoke, I can rationally deduce that there has to be a fire there. But then if smoke is the only thing I'm factoring in, my inference could be incorrect. The smoke could be from a large group of people having a cigar party. So then what is needed to maintain that rational proposition of "fire", is **more** empirical evidence. If I start walking towards the smoke, and I see things getting brighter and the air getting warmer, then the probability of my rational proposition, of there being a fire in the distance increases. Because now I have more empirical data in the form of light and heat to support my rational proposition. In other words, the more empirical evidence we have, the stronger the rational proposition becomes. Makes sense?

That's how "Sherlock Holmes" works. He uses empirical data to deduce rational propositions.

### Philosophical / Revelatory Propositions

The third type of propositions are the "philosophical or revelatory" propositions. These are the Quranic propositions that talk about عَالَمُ الْغَیْبِ- The Unseen Realm. It talks about the existence of a creator, angels, divine books, messengers, day of judgment, heaven and hell. That's basically what the Quran does. It offers Philosophical or Revelatory propositions. It proposes the idea of an afterlife, day of judgment, heaven and hell. These are the things we put our faith in. Our ایمان is grounded in these revelatory propositions. But how do we get there? How are we able to believe in these completely unseen, metaphysical propositions? Think about the best way possible to reveal the the hidden realm to intellectual human beings? The simplest way is to model or fashion a physical universe on the exact same principles as the spiritual universe! So that everything in that physical universe points back to the workings of the spiritual universe. That would be the most simple and efficient solution, right? Now the object that points to a destination is called what? It's called a sign! So Allah says:

سَنُرِيهِمْ آيَتِنَا فِى ٱلأٓفَاقِ وَفِىٓ أَنفُسِهِمْ

We will show them our signs, our evidence! Where? فِى ٱلأٓفَاقِ in the horizons. What do we find in the horizons? Empirical evidence! Everything we find outside of us exists in the form of observable data. Everything we see around us (that is physical in nature) are things we can see, hear, touch, taste and smell. وَفِىٓ أَنفُسِهِمْ And within their own selves. Because as rational human beings, we can deduce and infer rational propositions through empirical data! Therefore:

سَنُرِيهِمْ آيَتِنَا فِى ٱلأٓفَاقِ وَفِىٓ أَنفُسِهِمْ -

We will show them the evidence of the unseen realm in the form of empirical and rational data of this universe. How much evidence?

حَتَّىٰ يَتَبَيَّنَ لَهُمْ أَنَّهُ ٱلْحَقُّ

Unlimited evidence - until it becomes absolutely clear to them that these "revelatory" propositions are true.

So for example, Quran tells us that Allah is the creator of this universe. The fact that this universe is a "created" entity, is supported by our rational and empirical observations. Because we know there was a time in the past when this universe did not exist. We also know that there will come a time in the future when it will return back to non-existence. Existence and non-existence are both attributes of creation, therefore rationally speaking, this universe is a creation. Similarly, empirical data confirm that every creation necessitates the existence of a creator. So if this universe is a creation, it must also have a creator. So then when the Quran proposes:

اللہ الذی خلق السماوت و الارض

Allah is the one who created the heavens and the earth; that "revelatory proposition", gets backed up and supported by rational and empirical evidence found in the horizons that the human mind can understand and process. Now if we can believe this particular proposition, rationally, it makes it easier to believe when Allah says, that he also created a spiritual universe. Because logically speaking, the one who has the power to create this physical universe, must also have the power to create the spiritual universe.

So if you can truly understand this phenomenon, you can truly appreciate the motive of the Quran. This is why, everywhere in the Quran, Allah asks us to reflect on the "creations" of the heavens and the earth. He never asks us to reflect on Allah himself, right? Because that would be impossible for us to do without the empirical and rational data of this physical world. That's why he says:

إِنَّ فِى خَلْقِ ٱلسَّمَوَتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ وَٱخْتِلَفِ ٱلَّيْلِ وَٱلنَّهَارِ لَآيَتٍۢ لِّأُو۟لِى ٱلْأَلْبَبِ

No doubt about it, in the **creation** of the heavens and the earth, and the conflict between night and day, there are signs for the people of intellect. The use of the word لُب is very interesting here. Because it means a pith, or the seed, which is always hidden in the earth. This is why the intellect, is our vehicle of understanding because it can penetrate the unseen through the seen. It can deduce the existence of the hidden seed and the hidden roots, simply by observing the existence of the plant. We don't need to uproot and dig out the plant to conclude the rational proposition that it has roots. This is why Allah's demand to reflect on his creations!! Why? To gather the empirical and rational evidence that he created for our intellects in order to make it easier to believe the revelatory proposition. And this why Maulana Ali taught us:

لَا تَفَکَّرْ فِی اللہِ ..... ولاکن تفکر فی خلق اللہ

Don't reflect upon Allah; instead reflect upon the "creations" of Allah. Because in the witnessing of this universe, lies the evidence for what exists beyond it. The more we reflect on this physical creation, the more it will answer questions about the spiritual creation, and particularly its creator!

So the Quran proposes:

أَنَا اللَّـهُ الْعَزِيزُ الْحَكِيمُ

Indeed I am Allah! The most powerful and full of wisdom. You examine the universe and you will find the empirical and rational data to support that claim. Your study will reveal that this universe could only have been created by someone who is incredibly powerful and incredibly wise. Because there is purpose behind the existence of everything in creation. Therefore there has to be a purpose behind human creation!

The Quran proposes:

> ٱلرَّحْمَنُ ‎۱‏عَلَّمَ ٱلْقُرْآنَ ‎۲‏خَلَقَ ٱلْإِنسَنَ ‎۳‏عَلَّمَهُ ٱلْبَيَانَ

The intensely merciful taught the Quran, created man and taught him speech. A careful study of these acts of teaching the Quran, creating mankind and granting him the ability of speech; they will clearly prove that this could only be the act of a ٱلرَّحْمَنُ. Quran proposes:

اللَّهُ لا إِلَهَ إِلا هُوَ الْحَيُّ الْقَيُّومُ

Allah is the one that there is no deity worthy of worship except him. He is eternally living, and the ultimate maintainer of things. Empirical and rational data proves that you have to be alive in order to maintain. We know that all life on this earth is temporary; every creation will inevitably die. And if you're going to die, you will not be able to maintain; you will not be able to sustain and provide those that are dependent on you. Therefore only the ever-living can be the ultimate maintainer and sustainer. Which then supports the next revelatory proposition:

لا تَأْخُذُهُ سِنَةٌ وَلا نَوْمٌ

He never gets drowsy nor does he sleep. We know that drowsiness reduces are mental capabilities and focus. Sleep completely eliminates it for the time being. Therefore the one in a drowsy state, or an unconscious state, could not be considered all mighty, or the ultimate maintainer and sustainer. Because focus and vigilance is required for constant maintenance, right? And so if you look at the remaining propositions:

لَهُ مَا فِي السَّمَاوَاتِ وَمَا فِي الأَرْضِ

Everything in the heavens and earth belong to him

مَنْ ذَا الَّذِي يَشْفَعُ عِنْدَهُ إِلا بِإِذْنِهِ

Who can intercede on anyone's behalf without his permission

يَعْلَمُ مَا بَيْنَ أَيْدِيهِمْ وَمَا خَلْفَهُمْ وَلا يُحِيطُونَ بِشَيْءٍ مِنْ عِلْمِهِ إِلا بِمَا شَاءَ

He has complete knowledge of what lies ahead and what has come to past. And no one shares that knowledge except for those that he chose to share it with

وَسِعَ كُرْسِيُّهُ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالأَرْضَ وَلا يَئُودُهُ حِفْظُهُمَا

His throne sits over the heavens and the earth; he maintains control over the universe and that maintenance does not tire him out; it does not bore him out

وَهُوَ الْعَلِيُّ الْعَظِيمُ

And he is the all mighty and the all-exalted

If we carefully analyze and study these revelatory propositions in the light of the empirical and rational data that this universe provides, it becomes easy to believe in the very first statement, that: اللَّهُ لا إِلَهَ إِلا هُوَ Allah is the one and only deity worthy of my worship. Because no other creation in this universe, animate or inanimate fits that lofty description. Therefore, if I do choose an entity for worship, it only makes sense that I choose Allah.

So that's the first part of the formula - the design of the horizons, the آفاق, that provide the empirical and rational evidence to support the revelatory propositions offered by the Quran. They give us with a pathway to understand who our creator is, and the kinds of magnificent capabilities he possesses. Because the effects of his divine attributes reflect in his creation! This is the story of the Martian and the iPad from that session on Conclusions of Reflection.

### Self/Soul

The second part of the formula was the أنفس - the soul or the self. And what question does our soul or spirit answer? It answers the question of what Allah wants from us. How is that? To understand that, let's go back to that point in time when Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى decided to create the first soul - which was Adam AS. How did he describe his intentions to the angels? He said:

> وَإِذْ قَالَ رَبُّكَ لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ إِنِّى جَاعِلٌۭ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ خَلِيفَةًۭ ۖ قَالُوٓا۟ أَتَجْعَلُ فِيهَا مَن يُفْسِدُ فِيهَا وَيَسْفِكُ ٱلدِّمَآءَ وَنَحْنُ نُسَبِّحُ بِحَمْدِكَ وَنُقَدِّسُ لَكَ ۖ قَالَ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ
>
> And when your Lord said to the angels: "Verily, I am going to place generations after generations on earth." They said: "Will You place therein those who will make mischief therein and shed blood, - while we glorify You with praises and thanks and sanctify You." He said: "I know that which you do not know."

He said to the angels, I am going to create my caliph (my خلیفۃ) on this earth. Now let's understand the meaning of this word خلیفۃ. The word خلیفۃ comes from the root word خَلَفَ, which mean "back" or "behind". سَلَفَ is the antonym which means the ahead. So خلف means the one who is behind, or the one who follows; or the one who succeeds. In other words, خلیفۃmeans the representative. Allah said to the angels, I am planning to create my representative on earth. Now, think about this term - representative!

Caliph

![](images/81/9ce920c6-9fdc-45d1-a2a2-85e8cc4c7774.jpg)

A representative is a person chosen or appointed to act or speak on behalf of another person. Now the questions you have to ask is, if you had to choose someone to accurately represent you, what kind of traits would you want in that person? Someone who has the same or similar traits, if not the exact traits as you, right? Otherwise, how else would that person be able to represent you and your thoughts? This is why we learn in the story of creation of Adam, not only did Allah create Adam AS, he also taught him all his traits

> وَعَلَّمَ آدَمَ ٱلْأَسْمَآءَ كُلَّهَا ثُمَّ عَرَضَهُمْ عَلَى ٱلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ فَقَالَ أَنۢبِـُٔونِى بِأَسْمَآءِ هَٓؤُلَآءِ إِن كُنتُمْ صَدِقِينَ
>
> And He taught Adam all the names , then He showed them to the angels and said, "Tell Me the names of these if you are truthful."

He taught Adam all the names. He taught Adam all the divine traits. In other words, he trained Adam AS in all the divine traits that Allah himself possessed. Then he presented Adam AS to the angels and challenged them with those traits. What did the angels say?

> قَالُوا۟ سُبْحَنَكَ لَا عِلْمَ لَنَآ إِلَّا مَا عَلَّمْتَنَآ ۖ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلْعَلِيمُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ

The angels said: May you be glorified, we do not know anything other than what you have taught us. We have not received the training that Adam AS has received. Therefore we admit that we cannot be your true representatives on earth. That was their original argument remember?

> وَإِذْ قَالَ رَبُّكَ لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ إِنِّى جَاعِلٌۭ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ خَلِيفَةًۭ ۖ قَالُوٓا۟ أَتَجْعَلُ فِيهَا مَن يُفْسِدُ فِيهَا وَيَسْفِكُ ٱلدِّمَآءَ وَنَحْنُ نُسَبِّحُ بِحَمْدِكَ وَنُقَدِّسُ لَكَ ۖ قَالَ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ

Do you really want to create a representative who will spill blood and wreck havoc? We are the ones who are constantly praising and glorifying you. We are the ones who can represent you on earth; we are the ones who can be your true خلیفۃ- why do you need Adam for that? But once Allah created and trained Adam to reflect his divine attributes in character, they all accepted, and in a sense validated and confirmed, that indeed, it is only Adam, or mankind, that possesses the true capabilities to represent Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى on the earth.

This is the concept of Allah creating man in his own image. We possess all the attributes that our creator, our master possesses. We know Allah can see. He see's our actions and our deeds

وَٱللَّهُ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ بَصِيرٌۭ

We know Allah can hear

وَهُوَ ٱلسَّمِيعُ ٱلْعَلِيمُ

This is why he gave us the ability to hear so that we could accurately represent his qualities on this earth. But not just that, also be able to understand what he means when he says he watches our actions and hears our cries and supplications. You understand?

We know Allah can act, because we have the ability to act

كُلَّ يَوْمٍ هُوَ فِى شَأْنٍۢ

Every single day he is busy in work. We know Allah has the ability to love! Because the Quran says that he loves everything that is good, clean and pure

إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يُحِبُّ ٱلتَّوَّبِينَ وَيُحِبُّ ٱلْمُتَطَهِّرِينَ

This is why he gave us the ability to love! And this is why it is in our فطرۃ; our natural predisposition, to love clean and pure things. Because that is why our creator loves! You see that?

Similarly, we also know that Allah has the ability to hate and dislike

وَٱللَّهُ لَا يُحِبُّ ٱلْفَسَادَ

Allah hates or dislikes corruption. This is why he put it in our innate nature to dislike and be repelled and be disgusted with filth and foulness.

This is all because we have been created in God's image. We have been created with the same divine attributes that Allah possesses, so that we could be his true representatives, his true خلیفۃ on this earth. Why? So that we could truly come to know and understand our creator. You want to know what Allah wants from you? Its very simple! Just look at your own self?

وَفِىٓ أَنفُسِكُمْ ۚ أَفَلَا تُبْصِرُونَ

Why do you not reflect on your own self? Just see what you want for your own self, that is exactly what I want from you! SubhanAllah! Do you want justice? I want justice! Do you want to be loved? I want you to love me. Do you like to be praised? I like to be praised! Do you like it when people are grateful to you? I like it when my creation is grateful to me! Do you feel angry when people mess with your possessions and your rules? I get angry when you mess with my possessions and my rules! You want others to make you a priority in their lives? I want you to make me a priority in your life! You see?

Read the entire Quran cover to cover. There is not one thing that Allah demands from us in the Quran, that you and I would not demand from someone else. So if you want to know what Allah wants from you; all you have to do is reflect on your own self. What is it that you would want for your own self from other? That is exactly what I want from you. You want to be treated kindly? I want to be treated kindly. You want to be treated like you matter? I want to be treated like I matter to you. You want credit for the things you made? I'd like to be credited for the things I made - for making you and making this universe for you! SubhanAllah!

You ask questions about who created this universe? Ask yourself that question! Because I created you in my image! I created you with the same abilities I possess. I am خالق. I am the creator, I gave you the ability to create and construct. I made you خالق. Can even a needle exist in your world unless you create it? Then how can you even think, being created in my image, how can you even think that this universe came into existence without a creator?

You complain to me about the state of this world? You ask me, why don't I do something about it? I did! I created you! I created you in my own image! I gave you all my powers! Why aren't you doing something about it instead of complaining to me? You complain about poverty and misery, why don't you spend out of the wealth I gave you to make it better and easier with others? You talk about injustice, why don't you spend out of your courage that I gave you and fix those injustices! You see blind people, why don't you become their sight? You see weak people, why don't you become their strength? I gave you everything you need to fix the conditions of this world! But you do not want to step out of your comfort zones. All you want to do is complain, and have other people solve your problems for you. Well that's not how it's going to work! Because I created you as gods. I created you as الہ. Each and everyone of you has been created with the capabilities of an الہ so that you can recognize and identify the ال الہ - So that you can understand what your اللہ wants from you! SubhanAllah!

If you want to answer questions about what I want from you, all you have to do is look within your own self and study your own capabilities and desires, and you'll come to know what I want from you.

مَنْ عَرَفَ نَفْسَہُ فَقَدْ عَرَفَ رَبَّہُ

The one who comes to know of his own self, comes to know of his master! Because the slave has been created in the image of his master.

### Rights Of Allah

This opens up a world of reflection for all of us. There is so much to talk about on this topic that it's simply not possible for me to cover in these sessions Each and everyone of you is going to have to reflect on this and find answers for your own individual questions. But there is one thing I will talk about today in this context. I'll put one example in perspective for us - and that is the topic of "rights" - حقوق.

As human beings we have certain rights! We are entitled to certain fundamental rights. Based on what we've learnt today, we have to understand that Allah has rights. Just as we have certain human rights, Allah has certain divine rights. Human rights are based on divine rights; divine rights are not based on human rights. Which means that if human rights are important, then divine rights are a lot more important. One of the rights we have is the right to be treated with honor. After what a parent does for her child; after what a mother goes through to bring her child in this world, she gains a right of gratitude over the child. After the hard work a sincere and dedicated teacher puts in for her students, she gains a right of gratitude over her students; a right to be respected; a right to be treated with dignity and honor. Now if we as created beings, understand and appreciate the rights of human beings over one another; then what is the right of the one who created us all from nothing?

وَاللَّـهُ أَخْرَجَكُم مِّن بُطُونِ أُمَّهَاتِكُمْ لَا تَعْلَمُونَ شَيْئًا

What is the right of Allah who brought you out from the wombs of your mothers in a state where you knew nothing.

وَجَعَلَ لَكُمُ السَّمْعَ وَالْأَبْصَارَ وَالْأَفْئِدَةَۙ

What is the right of Allah who then gave you hearing and sight and a beating heart?

لَعَلَّكُمْ تَشْكُرُونَ

So that you could become grateful!

As human beings when we provide for someone, the least that we can expect back is their gratitude. A fair trade would be to expect the same or similar act of generosity back from them. But if they do not do that, then the very least we can expect from them is their gratitude. So then what about

لَا نَسْأَلُكَ رِزْقًاۖ نَّحْنُ نَرْزُقُكَۗوَ

We do not ask you for provisions; Indeed it is we that provide to you! So then what about that right of gratitude towards the one who takes nothing, and gives everything?

وَمَا بِكُم مِّن نِّعْمَةٍ فَمِنَ اللَّـهِۖثُمَّ

Any and every good thing that you have in your possession, has been given to you by Allah.

If we create something for a purpose; if we invent something for a purpose; as human beings we have the right to demand that it be used for the right purpose. We demand that it be used to serve the purpose it was created for. If not, we have the right to be upset! We have the right to demand justice. We have the right to sue the offender. Then what about the purpose of Allah's creation?

وَمَا خَلَقْتُ الْجِنَّ وَالْإِنسَ إِلَّا لِيَعْبُدُونِ

And indeed I have not created jinn and mankind except to worship me. How are we fulfilling that right of Allah? Does have the right to get upset and angry when we do not fulfill those rights?

We all have to really think about this divine formula that Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى has given us through this verse of

سَنُرِيهِمْ آيَتِنَا فِى ٱلأٓفَاقِ وَفِىٓ أَنفُسِهِمْ

We will show them our signs in the horizons and in their own selves, so that they can come to recognize who Allah is and what he wants from them. May Allah bless us all with the wisdom to fulfill his rights and obligations; and enable us to treat him with the honor and dignity he deserves! Because if anyone in this world treated us the way we treat Allah, I swear we would be extremely offended! Because we have been created in the image of the one who has ultimate rights. And that is all we truly are. An image! A Reflection of a distorted version of the divine attributes. We are not real. We are an illusion; just as the reflection in a mirror is a temporary illusion. We are here this moment; and we're gone the next. But Allah is real! His rights are real! His obligations are real!

I'll end the session with a beautiful story I heard recently. There was a man who heard about this very learned saint. He decided to go visit him and ask him for advise. So he travels a long distance and finally meets this saint. He greets him and says: I have heard that you are a great scholar and a ولی , a friend of Allah. Please give me some advise! The saint says to the man: My advise to you is, "If you do not consider Allah your superior, then at least consider him your equal". The man upon hearing this advise, says to himself, "What kind of a silly advise is this? Who would be so foolish as to not consider Allah his equal?! Is this what I traveled all the way for? I wasted my entire trip visiting this so called saint". Now he's thinking all this, and the saint is reading his thoughts. The saint says to him: You must be tired from your long journey. Why don't you spend the night in this inn and leave in the morning. The man agrees to that, and he spends the night in the inn. The next morning when he wakes up and offers his morning prayers, a servant comes to his door carrying a large tray. On that tray, one one side, there was fresh breakfast for the man. It had 4 eggs, 4 buttered toasts, 2 pieces of chicken, 2 glasses of orange juice. On the other side of the tray there was yesterdays food - some stale bread and lentils. Just as the man was about to eat, a beggar comes at the door, and calls out to the man! He says, "In the name of your creator I ask you for help. I haven't eaten for 2 days! For the love of your master, please give me something to eat". So the man gives the stale bread and lentils to the beggar and eats all the fresh food.

After breakfast, he gets ready to leave and decides to visit the saint in order to bid him farewell. He finds the saint and says his goodbyes. Just as he's about to leave, the saint says to him: My son! Remember my advise! "If you do not consider Allah your superior, then at least consider him your equal". The man loses it, and he yells at the saint: What kind of a stupid advise is this? Who in this world would be so foolish as to not consider Allah his superior. Even a child knows such a simple thing, and you're giving me such a ridiculous advise?!

The saint becomes furious at the man. He says listen! This morning when you sat down for breakfast, there was fresh food as well as stale food on your plate. A beggar came to you and asked you for food, in the name of your creator! He asked you for food in the love of your master! Had you considered Allah superior to yourself, you would have eaten the stale bread and lentils, and given the fresh food to the beggar! Had you considered Allah your equal, you would have shared your fresh food with the beggar! You would have eaten 2 eggs, and given 2 eggs to him. You would have eaten 2 toasts, and given 2 toasts to him. You would have enjoyed one glass of orange juice and let the beggar enjoy the other one. Oh wretched creation! Forget superior, you did not even consider Allah your equal!

I'll end today's session with the same advise to myself and everyone else: "If we do not consider Allah our superior, then considering his rights upon us, and remembering all his favors upon us, let us at least consider him our equal"

## The Horizons And The Soul

In the previous session we looked at the Quranic formula that allows us to gain some understand about who are creator is and what his intentions are. That Quranic formula is declared in the verse

Formula

![](images/86/718f2946-a675-46a9-ae87-0aaea1e7e6f8.jpg)

سَنُرِيهِمْ آيَتِنَا فِى ٱلأٓفَاقِ وَفِىٓ أَنفُسِهِمْ حَتَّىٰ يَتَبَيَّنَ لَهُمْ أَنَّهُ ٱلْحَقُّ ۗ أَوَلَمْ يَكْفِ بِرَبِّكَ أَنَّهُۥ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ شَهِيدٌ

We will show them Our Signs in the universe, and in their own selves, until it becomes absolutely clear to them that this is the truth. The signs in the horizons tell us about the nature and capabilities of Allah, whereas the signs in our souls tell us about the intentions of Allah; because Allah created us all in his own image, and he only demands for himself what we demand from others in terms of rights.

And this is how in these analogies between the ظاہر and the باطن - the horizons and the soul, we find insights into the inner workings of دین. Rasul Allah ﷺ said in a HADEES:

اِنَّا اللہ اَسَّسَ دِینَہُ عَلیَ مِثاَلِ خَلقِہِ لِیَستَدِلَّ بِالدِّینِ عَلَی الدُّنیَا ؛ وَ الدُّنیَا عَلَی الدِّین

Indeed Allah founded his religion on the pattern of creation, to provide evidence for his religion through the world; and to provide evidence for the world through his religion. In other words, he created the physical world on the exact same principles and patterns as the spiritual world. Why? So that the human intellect could study the physical, and then be able to relate those teachings with the spiritual. So horizons and the soul, or the self, are the keys to learning about Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى.

### Horizon - Refraction Of Attributes

He dispersed the knowledge of who he is in the horizons. A simple way to understand this concept is the example of light. If you look at light, we know that light is white. They call it white light, that does not have any color.

White Light

![](images/86/a4e369cc-73b8-4b20-ad70-e25c3f6e278e.jpg)

But what happens when you pass that light through a prism? We know that a prism is a medium of glass. When this white light hits this glass - it refracts; it bends. As a result of that, a whole spectrum of colors show up - from violate to red and everything in between. In the same way, when the light of Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى hits creation - because the Quran tells us that Allah is the light of the heavens and the earth:

اللہ نور السموت والارض

Divine Light

![](images/86/c6127d05-d962-40d3-b020-fef39599aca6.jpg)

So what happens is, when this pure light of Allah's **essence **hits creation; when it enters the realm of physicality, it refracts into the spectrum of these divine attributes. This is why we get all of these different manifestations of Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى's essence in this universe. You see? This عالم is the prism that is manifesting Allah's various attributes in creations for us to observe. So in one instance we see that Allah is کریم He is generous; and in another instance the universe shows us that he is جبار (the compeller). In another instance the عالم reveals to us that he is قھار (the subduer); in another instance we see that he is عزیز (the powerful); in another instance he is مُزِل (the humiliator); In another he is معز (the bestower of honor). So the عالم essentially serves as colossal theater, for the divine manifestations of these attributes of Allah - through which we come to know about him.

Why did he set the stage up this way? Because as I mentioned earlier, the human intellect learns by defining and limiting things; just as the body works by breaking down food. The intellect, like the body, in order to digest knowledge, has to take in the whole, break it down into parts, and then understand each part individually. Only then it can sum those parts up again to understand the whole. The عالم presents us the parts in the form of these divine attributes, so that we can reflect on them individually and then sum them up to put together a more complete understanding of our creator. This is the essential theme of Quran. It invites us to reflect on the heavens and the earth; the horizons and the souls - because through that very reflection, we will recognize our creator and his abilities. And then it turns our attention inward on to the soul (the نفس), to understand the purpose of our own creation! What is the purpose of our creation?

### Soul: Purpose Of Creation

Scholars state that there are 3 reasons for the creation of human beings- and what's beautiful about this distinction is that it allows for purposeful existence for not just Muslims but for people of other beliefs as well; or even for people without belief.

Purpose of creation

![](images/86/b55d6057-fdd9-410d-84b2-9b42960d764a.jpg)

The first one he says is for عمارۃ. Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى said:

هُوَ أَنشَأَكُم مِّنَ ٱلْأَرْضِ وَٱسْتَعْمَرَكُمْ فِيهَا

We brought you forth from the earth to cultivate it. The second reason he says, is عبادۃ

وَمَا خَلَقْتُ ٱلْجِنَّ وَٱلْإِنسَ إِلَّا لِيَعْبُدُونِ

We only created the jinn (which are beings of the unseen realm) and humans (beings of the seen realm) for عبادۃ for willing worship. ابن عباس said in a narration that the purpose behind the creation of human beings was also to لیعرفون - to know God; and to know god is to love god. Those of you who heard my session on stages of love, know that عبودیۃ is one of the levels of محبۃ - of love. It is one of the higher level of love. Therefore, he created the jinn and the humans to love him.

The third and last reason for the creation of human beings is خلافۃ:

وَيَسْتَخْلِفَكُمْ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ فَيَنظُرَ كَيْفَ تَعْمَلُونَ

We made you خلفاء because the خلیفۃ, The caliph is the one who stands in place of somebody else. He is the one who is left behind - ُخَلَفَہ. He is left behind to stand in the place of someone else. In other words a خلیفۃ is a steward. Allah created us as his stewards on this earth. Why? فَيَنظُرَ كَيْفَ تَعْمَلُونَ, so that he can witness what we do with all the external and internal gifts he blessed us with.

Now there are 2 types of خلافۃ. We have been made a caliph over ourselves, and then over others. For the خلافۃ of the self Allah said:

وَإِذْ قَالَ رَبُّكَ لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ إِنِّى جَاعِلٌۭ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ خَلِيفَةًۭ

Allah said to the angles, I have decided to create a caliph in the earth. In سُورَۃُ الصَّوَادِ He said to داود علیہ السلام . He said:

Sad [38:26]

> يَدَاوُۥدُ إِنَّا جَعَلْنَكَ خَلِيفَةًۭ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ فَٱحْكُم بَيْنَ ٱلنَّاسِ بِٱلْحَقِّ وَلَا تَتَّبِعِ ٱلْهَوَىٰ فَيُضِلَّكَ عَن سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ ۚ إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ يَضِلُّونَ عَن سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ لَهُمْ عَذَابٌۭ شَدِيدٌۢ بِمَا نَسُوا۟ يَوْمَ ٱلْحِسَابِ

Oh داؤد! We have made you a caliph in the earth. This is the second type of خلافۃ that is mentioned in the Quran. So there are two types of خلافۃ. The first one is to rule yourself; and the other is to rule others! But the condition of ruling others, is that you rule yourself first! That you take over control and mastery of your نفس first. And so here داؤد is being told, we have put you in the earth to rule over others - he was made a ruler over بَنِی اِسْرَائِیلَ; so that

فَٱحْكُم بَيْنَ ٱلنَّاسِ بِٱلْحَقِّ ; you judge among them with the truth and then he says:

وَلَا تَتَّبِعِ ٱلْهَوَىٰ ; do not follow your caprice! In other words, you have to rule yourself first, before you can rule others. Because if you don't do that

فَيُضِلَّكَ عَن سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ , the ف here is for causation; because if you do not! If you follow your caprice, your whims, your appetites, then it will lead you astray from the path of Allah.

So what are we as Caliphs of Allah? And how are we doing as Allah's representatives on earth? Allah says:

سَنُرِيهِمْ آيَتِنَا فِى ٱلأٓفَاقِ وَفِىٓ أَنفُسِهِمْ حَتَّىٰ يَتَبَيَّنَ لَهُمْ أَنَّهُ ٱلْحَقُّ ۗ أَوَلَمْ يَكْفِ بِرَبِّكَ أَنَّهُۥ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ شَهِيدٌ

We will show them Our Signs in the horizons, and in our own souls. Allah will let us know through signs of what we see around us - our own truth and realities! So let's look at some of the signs that we see in our horizons today to reflect on who we are and how we're doing as caliphs of Allah; just as a physician would look at the symptoms of an ill patient. Allah says in سُورَۃُ الرُّومِ

The Romans [30:41]

> ظَهَرَ ٱلْفَسَادُ فِى ٱلْبَرِّ وَٱلْبَحْرِ بِمَا كَسَبَتْ أَيْدِى ٱلنَّاسِ لِيُذِيقَهُم بَعْضَ ٱلَّذِى عَمِلُوا۟ لَعَلَّهُمْ يَرْجِعُونَ

Mischief, corruption, pollution has appeared on the land and sea, because of what the hands of men have earned. لِيُذِيقَهُم , the لام there is again for causation

لِيُذِيقَهُم بَعْضَ ٱلَّذِى عَمِلُوا۟ لَعَلَّهُمْ يَرْجِعُونَ so that Allah can give them a taste of their deed,s in order for them to turn back. So the reason that Allah makes our negative actions have negative repercussions, is in order for us to return back to Allah.

Scholars say that ‏ظَهَرَ ٱلْفَسَادُ - is actually النقص فِى ٱلْبَرِّ وَٱلْبَحْرِ. It means the diminished blessings of the earth. You lose the blessings of the earth; and it means also القحط which is droughts and famines and all the harms that come in agriculture. The fruits of the land - lands that stop growing crops. It also means the benefits of the oceans will diminish - from the fish and other animals that live in the oceans - like the whales. And this is going to happen because of what we earn from our sinfulness. Because human beings, even though created in the best of forms - in the very image of its creator; with all his divine attributes, is capable of extreme corruption when he deviates from his divinity; when he deviates from his original state. This is that very concern that the angels expressed when Allah mentioned the creation of Adam to them. They said

قَالُوٓا۟ أَتَجْعَلُ فِيهَا مَن يُفْسِدُ فِيهَا وَيَسْفِكُ ٱلدِّمَآءَ

Are you seriously considering creating a species therein who will create فساد and spill blood? The word فساد , if you look at it in the English language, it means rottenness, corruption, decay, decomposition, putrid, depravity, wickedness, viciousness, inequity, immorality, weakness, perversion, wrongness, incorrectness. All of these, are meanings embodied in that word فساد. If you look at the word pollution in the English dictionary, it says: Undesirable state of the natural environment being contaminated with harmful substances as consequences of human activities.

بِمَا كَسَبَتْ أَيْدِى ٱلنَّاسِ - This is the result! So even in our language, pollution is a dominant problem on the planet.

We as human beings, are responsible for the present state of the earth today. Our conditions of hardships in the form of famines and extreme weathers, and earthquakes and floods are nothing other than بِمَا كَسَبَتْ أَيْدِى ٱلنَّاسِ - from what our own hands have earned. Many countries are in major crisis because of fires that were started in forests. Climate changes are driving up temperatures; and as temperatures rise, there are more incidents of fires.

Another aspect of this فساد is the air itself. In India it has been estimated that half a million people die from air pollution. We had 15000 people die from terrorism - and the US government moved heaven and earth to have this war on terrorism - yet half a million people suffer and die from air pollution - where's the war against that? Noise pollution! In Pakistan and India, look at the levels of noise pollution in the form of honking and yelling and screaming and the noise from rickshaws and taxis with broken silencers. That's all man made فساد.

Today we know that the world is rapidly moving towards desertification. This is because we're losing what they call top-soil. Desertification is literally where a place that once had foliage becomes desert. It has already happened in large portions of fertile lands and it continues to grow. I was reading this book in which it said that there was a time, when all of north Africa was so fertile, that before بنو حِلاؤ got into Africa, a squirrel could run from سنجاب, from Egypt all the way to Morocco without its feet ever touching the ground. The land was so fertile! But when the بنو حلاؤ came, they were like locus! They completely devoured the resources. So desertification is another example of بِمَا كَسَبَتْ أَيْدِى ٱلنَّاسِ - from what our own hands have earned

Another aspect of فساد is as Allah says:

وَٱلْأَرْضَ وَضَعَهَا لِلْأَنَامِ

The earth has been put out for the انام. The word انام comes from نوم which means sleep. أنام are creatures that sleep. So this verse is about all sentient beings - it is not just for human beings. It includes the animal kingdom. Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى

> وَمَا مِن دَآبَّةٍۢ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ وَلَا طَٓئِرٍۢ يَطِيرُ بِجَنَاحَيْهِ إِلَّآ أُمَمٌ أَمْثَالُكُم ۚ مَّا فَرَّطْنَا فِى ٱلْكِتَبِ مِن شَىْءٍۢ ۚ ثُمَّ إِلَىٰ رَبِّهِمْ يُحْشَرُونَ
>
> There is not a moving creature on earth, nor a bird that flies with its two wings, but are communities like you. We have neglected nothing in the Book, then unto their Lord they shall be gathered.

وَمَا مِن دَآبَّةٍۢ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ وَلَا طَٓئِرٍۢ يَطِيرُ بِجَنَاحَيْهِ إِلَّآ أُمَمٌ أَمْثَالُكُم

That there is no creature on the earth that walks the earth; there is no bird that flies with its two wings, except that they have communities like unto you. Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى tells us that the animal kingdoms have communities just like us. Then he says he did not leave anything out from this book

مَّا فَرَّطْنَا فِى ٱلْكِتَبِ مِن شَىْءٍۢ

Even the animals are acknowledged in the Quran. We know that there are animals that mate for life. We know that animals make huge pilgrimage and vast migrations to places, just like we do. Today, because of the sins of mankind, dogs are on prosaic. In the United States they put dogs on prosaic - which are anti-depressants. Because they are depressed in the houses of depressed and miserable people. Dogs are not naturally depressed animals. But there are so many houses in the US that are sick, that the dogs and the cats themselves get sick - because they are sentient beings. They are emotional beings. They feel and experience emotions just like we do.

Look at the problem of endangered species caused by the hands of man. There are 25 critically endangered species of the world that are from the most beautiful and most majestic animals. These are animals that the Ancient Arabs used to name their children after! Animals like lions and tigers - أسد and فھد . These are animals that the Arabs - they named their men after مُفْتَرِسَات - after the animals that attack their prey! And they named their women after the prey itself like ماھا and غزالۃ. So فھد goes after ماھا and she doesn't stand a chance!!

But what's interesting, is that if you look at the species that are endangered today, they are not the cockroaches! They are not the rats! They are not the snakes! They are flourishing! The animals and species that are endangered are the ones that represent the divine qualities within us that are disappearing! The eagle, the peregrine, the lion, the tiger, the elephants - these animals represent the great qualities in human beings that are disappearing!

وَفِىٓ أَنفُسِكُمْ ۚ أَفَلَا تُبْصِرُونَ

Within your own souls, why don't you reflect?! We've got plenty of cockroaches out there in human disguise! We have plenty of rats and snakes out there in human disguise! But we no longer find the courage of lions, and the majesty of the eagles in human form. These qualities are extinct from human beings!

I can go on an on about this; and I'm sure you can think of and add much more! But this is what we're doing as the caliphs and the stewards of Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى. We are destroying the very earth that sustains us and provides us with our livelihood! And then we complain that we have problems! Why doesn't Allah solve all our problems! Why should he? He's not our janitor! He's not our servant! He is not our errand boy! He created us in his image so that we could create جنۃ on earth just as he created جنۃ on heaven! Why else do you think Adam was created in جنۃ ? Just for fun? No! He was created in جنۃ so that he could see and experience the beauty of that place so that when he comes to the earth, he could cultivate the land and create that same جنۃ on earth!

وَ أَنشَأَكُم مِّنَ ٱلْأَرْضِ وَٱسْتَعْمَرَكُمْ فِيهَا

He brought you forth from the earth to cultivate it. To create that same جنۃ on earth! And what did we do? And why did this happen? How did things get so bad and out of hand? Because of **our** corruption. Because of our فساد , our deviation from the divine attributes and majestic qualities that Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى created us with. This is why he created the عالمین for us - so that we can study the signs of horizons - the heavens and the earth - to learn the ideal condition of the نفس - the ideal condition of the soul, through his divine attributes.

But we as human beings, as خَلِیفَۃُ اللَّہِ have failed miserably! And we have proven that the angels did have valid grounds for their concern! They clearly knew what they were talking about when they said:

أَتَجْعَلُ فِيهَا مَن يُفْسِدُ فِيهَا وَيَسْفِكُ ٱلدِّمَآءَ

Do you wish to create someone who will sow corruption on earth and spill blood? They clearly knew what they were talking about.

So what is it that we have deviated from? We have deviated from the divine attributes - from the character of Allah!

### AKHLAQ

Character

![](images/86/ce70c60c-96bc-46d3-9609-28618152e57f.jpg)

The word أخلاق comes from the word خُلق. And this is really interesting, because we are called خَلق. The word خَلق means creation, which is related to the body that is related to the horizons; and خُلق means character which belongs to the soul. They're both made up of the same letters and in the same sequence. Therefore according to the science of Arabic, both these words are closely related in meaning. The word خَلقis related to the body which is nourished by this physical world . The word خُلق is character, which takes its nourishment from the spiritual world. خَلق represents the existence of the body; and خُلق represents the existence of the soul. Both these words begin with the letter خ, which is a sound of friction in Arabic. It is a sound of effort. This is because the body takes effort to develop, and so does character. Character takes a lot of effort to develop. Therefore if خَلق is the essence of the body, then خُلق or character is the essence of the soul. When we die, the body returns back into the earth with whatever it has acquired from the physical world. And the soul returns back to the spiritual world with its nourishment of character that it has developed in the world. That's our true earning. That is our real acquisition خُلق, character! A soul that returns back to its master without a developed خُلق is an impoverished soul. It is a soul that has failed the purpose of its خلقۃ; it has failed the purpose of its creation. When the body is weak, it cannot benefit from the offerings of this physical world. We know that this world has a lot to offer in terms of temporal pleasures; but if you're extremely weak, you can't even get up off your bed, you'll not be able to enjoy those pleasures. In the same way a soul that is empty; that is bankrupt of أخلاق is not going to be able to benefit from the pleasures of the after life.

How is character developed? Through revelation! As I said, the Quran, in an oversimplified statement, is the explanation of divine attributes of Allah. It explains the attributes of Allah and why he has those attributes. He is عظیم, because he created this massive universe. He is قوی . All powerful because not only did he create this universe، he created it with one single command. کُن. Be and فیکون . it was. So in essence Quran is just the description of Allah's attributes explaining why he is ال الہ. Our hearts, witness these divine attributes in the light of revelation, which then creates **Belief**! The more the heart understands revelation, the more we grow in our belief - which then nourishes and develops our character. And so character is nothing more than absorbing these divine attributes into the heart from revelation, and then reflecting those same divine attributes into the rest of Allah's creation. That is what character is! It is internalizing, developing, and training the heart in the light of these divine attributes; and then reflecting them in our interactions with Allah's creation.

### Interactions In Islam

That is أخلاق and that is Islam. Islam is all about Interactions. It is معاملات. There are 3 types of interactions in Islam

1. Interaction with Allah - حُقُوقُ اللَّہِ

1. Interaction with the self - حُقُوقُ النَّفْسِ

1. Interaction with the creation of Allah - حقوق العباد

Huqooq Allah

![](images/86/d8bc7f29-420b-4720-94a6-45362c80bbb2.jpg)

What right does Allah have over us? That we recognize his attributes in the heavens and the earth, and we come to love him, for everything he has done for us! And then out of that love, we willingly obey and serve him as faithful slaves - as عبد. That we worship him recognizing the fact that only he deserves to be worshiped! That is the right of the creator over his creation. What is the right of the self?

Huqooq Nafs

![](images/86/097a919e-1633-48a4-9509-d2ca569658fb.jpg)

To reflect on the attributes of Allah in the عالم, through the teachings of Quran. To learn the attributes, the qualities, the character of our master, and to then internalize and harvest those qualities within our own hearts to develop our character; our أخلاق. Because we have been created in the image of our master. We have been created to represent our master on this earth - which means we have to adopt the qualities and characteristics of our master! That is what a خلیفۃ is - a representative! You can't represent unless you become like the one you are representing!

So for instance, Allah is کریم. He is generous. Therefore it is the right of my soul that I train it to be generous. What is the greatest generosity that I can show myself? To protect it from sins, and put it to work in good deeds. Allah is الحق, he is truthful! it is the right of my soul that I train it in being truthful. With who? Truthful with myself. What is the greatest falsehood that the self is trapped in? This delusion of the reality of دنیا. Therefore I must train myself to wake up from my delusions about this world. I need to wake up to the reality that I am only here for a short period of time. Everything that I'm working for in the worldly sense will be left behind. I will be left with nothing other than the title of میت in this world when I die. My loved ones, the people that I am slaving for day and night, they will be the first to dump me in a grave and throw dirt in my face. So I better not sacrifice my afterlife for this temporary life. That's being truthful to the self. So the right of the self, is that I internalize the attributes of Allah, and train myself in those attributes in order to develop my character, so that I can realize the potential I was created with. I was created in the image of god, to act like God. And God is nothing other than just! This is why one of the divine names of Allah is المؤمن, which essentially means - the giver of tranquility. The one who brings peace - comes from the root امن which means peace.

Huqooq Ibaad

![](images/86/951440aa-8e7c-46e7-a2f8-04c4aed6c38b.jpg)

And finally there is the right of others. What are the right of others? What are حقوق العباد.

Right of Allah, was recognizing his divine attributes in creation, and submitting to him willingly and out of love in عبادۃ. Right of the self, was to internalize those divine attributes to build our character, aligning it with the character of Allah. To become like the creator! Once that character has developed, only then the final right of حقوق العباد comes into play, which is the idea of reflecting those divine attributes of Allah in interactions within his creation!

We trained ourselves to be generous with our own selves; now it's time to reflect that generosity on to others in our dealings with them. We trained ourselves to be truthful and sincere with our own selves. Now it's time to reflect that truthfulness. that صدق, that sincerity in our dealings with others. We've recognized how Allah is forgiving with us; how he is patient with us. We now have to reflect those attributes by being patient and forgiving towards others.

This is not an easy task! This is why one has to go through the training of fulfilling the previous 2 rights before he can fulfill this 3rd right! A man once came to the messenger of Allah and said.

یَا رَسُولَ اللہِ. اَصِلُ اَخِیہِ وَ یَقطَعُنِی ' وَ اُعطِیہِ وَ یَمنَعُنِی

Oh messenger of Allah, I give him but he withholds from me. I come to him but he cuts me off. What should I do? the Prophet replied, that is صلۃ الرحم. What you doing is the right thing! Continue meeting him even though he is cutting you off. That's Islam! This is Islam. And its hard work. Don't think this is an easy religion. But we want to be people that strive! We want to be people that honor ourselves, because Allah honored us when he said:

وَلَقَدْ كَرَّمْنَا بَنِىٓ آدَمَ وَحَمَلْنَهُمْ فِى ٱلْبَرِّ وَٱلْبَحْرِ

We have ennobled the children of Adam. We've made you leaders over the land and the oceans. We gave you a heart. We gave you an intellect. We nourish them with our revelation. We give you the ability to overcome the worst aspect of yourself; and even if you capitulate from time to time, we've given you the means to renew and restore your soul through توبۃ , through repentance. What a gift from Allah! So this is what we have to do, and we have to do it with أخلاص. with sincerity! Only for the sake of Allah. But it's not easy.

3 Dimensions Of Islam

![](images/86/0f665992-7d40-40ca-833b-ab1b223125cd.jpg)

So these are the 3 dimensions of Islam. The rights of Allah, the rights of the self and the rights of others. Recognizing the attributes of Allah in the heavens and the earth help us understand the rights of Allah (حُقُوقُ اللَّہِ). Internalizing those divine attributes to build character is the right of the soul (حُقُوقُ النَّفْسِ). Reflecting those divine attributes on to the creation of Allah is the right of others (حُقُوقُ الْعِبَاد). And because this last one is the most challenging of all, it cannot be accomplished unless and until the soul itself has been illuminated with the character of Allah. This is why Rasul Allah ﷺ said in a HADEES:

أتخذو من أخلاق اللہ

Adopt the character and the qualities of Allah!

There is a narration in which Rasul Allah once saw a man talking to Maulana Ali in a very disrespectful manner. In in return, Maulana Ali responded back to him with kindness. Despite his kindness, this man continued to act in a disrespectful and manner with him. Yet Maulana Ali kept responding with kindness over and over again. When the man left, the prophet asked him, یا علی! I saw this man talk to you in such a disrespectful manner, treat you in a horrible way - over and over again. Yet you kept showing him kindness and respect in return. Why is that? Maulana Ali replied: یَا رَسُولَ اللہِ If this man is not willing to give up his wretched and evil nature to interact with me, then why should I give up my good character to interact with him? SubhanAllah! What a beautiful statement! If this man is not willing to give up his wretched and evil nature to interact with me, then why should I give up my good character to interact with him? In other words, if this man continues to hold on to something ugly, why should I let go of something beautiful that I possess. That's the beauty of أخلاق.

This is why we follow the example of Rasul Allah ﷺ who according to Quran reach the pinnacle of اخلاق. Through his character, and nothing but his character, he purified the most barbaric and the most vile group of people that ever existed. Read the history of Islam. Understand the mentality of the people he was sent to reform. They were worst than animals. There was not a single virtue that existed within them. They would eat the flesh of elephants and crocodiles. When they ate a goat, they even boiled and ate its leather. They would burn their enemies alive. They would rip out their skin and wear it like a scarf. They would crack open their skull and use it as a cup to drink wine in. Their women would dye their clothes in the blood of their enemies. Not only would they kill the enemy. they would tie that persons camel to his grave and starve it to death. Sons would marry their mothers; Daughters were buried alive. They would use the children of their enemies for target practice. Even their prayers were were barbaric.

یا ربِّ فَلا تَجعَل حَیَاتی مَذِلَّۃُ ولا مَوتَتِی بَینَ النِّسآءِ نوائِحِ

ولاکن قَتیلاً تَمزَقُ الطَّیرِ حَولَہُ . و تَشرُبُ غِربانُ الدَّم من جوارح

Oh lord, do not disgrace me in my life by letting me die on a bed between grieving women
Give me a death, where birds are tearing out my flesh and crows are drinking the fresh blood gushing out of my limbs.

This was the state of the Arabs when Rasul Allah came between them! This is the group he was sent to reform! And look what he did! He converted these people in to رحمۃ اللہ علیہ. We don't even mention their names without saying رحمۃ اللہ علیہ- May the mercy of Allah be with them. This is why on the day of غدیر when Rasul Allah asked:

اَلَسْتُ اَوْلَاکُمْ مِنْ اَنْفُسِکُمْ؟

Am I not more beloved to you than your own souls? What did they reply.

بلیٰ یَا رَسُولَ اللہِ

Indeed you are oh prophet of Allah

كنا الحيوانات! كُنتُ جعلنا البشر

We were animals! You made us human beings.

So how did he do that? Through his impeccable اخلاق. By internalizing the divine attributes of his creator, by internalizing them to develop an immaculate character, and finally reflecting those very same attributes in creation - with such intensity, that the creator himself acknowledged:

وَإِنَّكَ لَعَلَىٰ خُلُقٍ عَظِيمٍۢ

Indeed! No doubt about it, You have achieved the utmost, and the most exalted level of character!

## The Womb Of Afterlife

Now that we understand these meanings of رب and الرحمن and الرحیم, I want to share with you a beautiful spiritual representation that Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى has created for us through the example of the the womb. To quickly reiterate, a رب is a combination of 6 things. In classical Arabic

الرَبُّ ھُوَ المَالِکُ وَ السَّیِّد وَ المُرَبِّی وَ المُرْشَدُ وَ المُنعِمُ وَ القَیِّمُ

He is an owner and the one with complete authority over his property. He is the one who nurtures his creation, guides it, benefits it by bestowing generous and undeserving gifts upon it; and he is the one who maintains it. That is who Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى is. That's his primary role with his creation. All his other attributes center around and stem from his ربوبیۃ. So if someone asks, "Who is Allah?", the answer is that "Allah is رب". Now as for his nature, his nature is الرحمن and الرحیم. The manner in which he carries out the functions of his ربوبیۃ is through intense and consistent love, compassion and mercy.

### Womb is the first master

So now that we understand these concepts, I want to share this example that Allah سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى has created for us through the womb. We can understand a lot about our master's plan for us, by studying the relationship of the womb with its fetus. Because the womb is the رب of the fetus and it demonstrates the exact same principles of رحمنیۃ and رحیمیۃ with it. There is a HADEES in which Rasul Allah ﷺ said:

قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّہِ صَلَّی اللَّہُ عَلَیْہِ وَ آلِہِ وَ سَلَّمَ

> الدُّنْیا رَحْمُ الآخِرَۃ
>
> This world is a womb for the afterlife

In other words, the story of our lives in this world, tells a tale about the kind of life we're going to have in the afterlife. Because this world (this دنیا) that we're living in, is the womb and we are the fetus in that womb. Just as the brief and temporary residence in a womb is preparation for this worldly life; our stay in this temporal world is nothing more than a duration of preparation for the after life.

If you look at the رحم - the womb, you'll see that all 6 principles of ربوبیۃ apply. It has ownership and authority over the embryo. It nurtures the embryo. It helps it grow and develop into a fetus. So the womb مالک, it is سید and it is also مربی. Then we see that the womb bestows priceless gifts on the fetus through the development of its eyes, its nose, ears, hands and feet; the heart, the intellect and all of its internal and external organs. So the womb is also the منعم. We also know the womb manages the enter development process of the embryo from its conception until the time it is fully developed. So the womb is also a قیم. In other words, the womb acts as the very first physical رب that we come into contact with; and the very first act this design demonstrates, is the act of ربوبیۃ by carrying out the 6 acts of:

مَالِکُ وَ السَّیِّد وَ المُرَبِّی وَ المُرْشَدُ وَ المُنعِمُ وَ القَیِّمُ

Now what happens once the baby is delivered into this world? Who is the baby handed over to? Its loving mother who becomes the next immediate رب of that baby - not the father! the mother. It's the mother that becomes the next رب why? Because she feeds it. So there is a transference of ربوبیۃ that takes place.
